Core BMM kernel with same h grid:
```
@triton.jit
def core_bmm_kernel(L_ptr, R_ptr, C_ptr, B, N, H,
                    BLOCK_I: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_J: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    num_j = N // BLOCK_J
    num_i = N // BLOCK_I
    # decode h/i/j: h fastest? For L2 maybe i/j tiles then h? Use h fastest to have consecutive programs same i,j different h? A/B loads for different h not same but contiguous. Maybe i fastest? Standard group scheduling? We can use 1D: pid = ((b*H + h)*num_i + i)*num_j + j? Could decode.
```
Need grid = B*H*num_i*num_j.
Optimal schedule for L2? For GEMM, program order affects L2 reuse of A/B. If h slowest? A_h for different h not reused. For same h, A tile reused across j; B across i. We want programs with same h and adjacent i/j close. If decode `j` fastest, then for fixed h,i, all j programs run consecutively: they share A tile and load different B. Good. Then i next share B? Maybe. `h` slowest. Use decode:
```
pid_j = pid % num_j
pid_ij = pid // num_j
pid_i = pid_ij % num_i
pid_bh = pid_ij // num_i
h = pid_bh % H
b = pid_bh // H
```
Thus j fastest, i next, h next. Good. For N=1024, 8 i, 8 j => 64 programs per h share row/col. Nice.

Offsets:
`offs_i = pid_i * BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_I)`
`offs_j = pid_j * BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_J)`
`offs_k = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)`
A base: L_ptr + b*N*H*N + i_start*H*N + h*N (plus k)
B base: R_ptr + b*N*H*N + j_start*H*N + h*N
C base: C_ptr + b*H*N*N + h*N*N + i_start*N + j_start
Loop for k0 in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
```
a = tl.load(a_ptrs) # maybe no mask
r = tl.load(r_ptrs)
acc = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(r), acc)
a_ptrs += BLOCK_K
r_ptrs += BLOCK_K
```

Final kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def finalize_kernel(c_ptr, og_ptr, w_ptr, gw_ptr, gb_ptr, out_ptr,
                    B, N, H, C, eps,
                    BLOCK_P: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    n_n = N*N
    num_pb = n_n // BLOCK_P # if divisible
    pb = pid % num_pb
    b = pid // num_pb
    offs_p = pb*BLOCK_P + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_P)
    global_p = b*n_n + offs_p
    off_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
    if H == BLOCK_H? use H_EQ128?
```
Triton `if H == BLOCK_H` with H runtime? It can evaluate but not compile-time? H is int arg, not constexpr, but Python if in JIT may not be allowed? It can lower to runtime branch? I think `if` on runtime scalar is allowed? But for static specialization, use `H_IS_128: tl.constexpr` passed. Host sets `H_IS_128 = (H == 128)`. In kernel:
```
if H_IS_128:
   off_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
   # assume H=128
   c_tile = tl.load(c_ptr + b*H*n_n + off_h[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:])
   cf = c_tile.to(tl.float32)
   sums = tl.sum(cf, axis=0)
   sumsq = tl.sum(cf*cf, axis=0)
   mean = sums / 128.0
   var = sumsq / 128.0 - mean*mean
   var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
   rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)
   gamma = tl.load(gw_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)
   beta = tl.load(gb_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)
   c_pt = tl.trans(cf)
   og = tl.load(og_ptr + global_p[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:]).to(tl.float32)
   sig = 1.0/(1.0+tl.exp(-og))
   gated = (c_pt - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None] * gamma[None,:] + beta[None,:]
   gated = gated * sig
   gated_bf = gated.to(tl.bfloat16)
   # loop c blocks
   offs_c = 0
   for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
      oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
      # If C may not multiple? assume.
      w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:]).to(tl.bfloat16) # [BC,H]
      w = tl.trans(w_ct) # [H,BC]
      acc = tl.dot(gated_bf, w)
      tl.store(out_ptr + global_p[:,None]*C + oc[None,:], acc)
else:
   # stats pass
   sums = tl.zeros((BLOCK_P,), dtype=tl.float32)
   sumsq = ...
   for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
      oh = h0 + off_h
      valid_h = oh < H
      base = c_ptr + b*H*n_n + oh[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:]
      # load with mask valid_h[:,None] (p no mask)
      ct = tl.load(base, mask=valid_h[:,None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
      sums += tl.sum(ct, axis=0)
      sumsq += tl.sum(ct*ct, axis=0)
   mean = sums / H_F
   var = ...
   for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
       acc = zeros(BLOCK_P, BLOCK_C)
       oc = ... c valid? assume
       for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
          oh=...
          valid_h=...
          base=...
          ct=load...
          c_pt=tl.trans(ct) # [BP, BH]
          gamma=load(gw+oh, mask=valid_h, other=0).to(fp32)
          beta=...
          og=load(og_ptr + global_p[:,None]*H + oh[None,:], mask=valid_h[None,:], other=0)
          sig=...
          norm = (c_pt - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None] * gamma[None,:] + beta[None,:]
          gated=(norm*sig).to(bf16)
          w_ct=load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + oh[None,:], mask=valid_h[None,:], other=0).to(bf16) # [BC,BH]
          w=tl.trans(w_ct)
          acc += tl.dot(gated, w)
       store acc to out + oc
```

Pseudo:

```
@triton.jit
def layer_norm_kernel(x_ptr, w_ptr, b_ptr, out_ptr, C, inv_C, eps,
                      BLOCK_P: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    n_chunks? M = out_ptr shape? Need total rows M = B*N*N. Pass M.
    offs_p = pid*BLOCK_P + arange(BLOCK_P)
    p_valid = offs_p < M
    offs_c = arange(BLOCK_C)
    c_valid = offs_c < C
    mask = p_valid[:,None] & c_valid[None,:]
    x = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_p[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_C
    xm = x - mean[:,None]
    var = tl.sum(xm*xm, axis=1) * inv_C
    rstd = 1/sqrt(var+eps)
    # Need gamma/beta: load C values? BLOCK_C with mask.
    gamma = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    beta = tl.load(b_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    y = xm * rstd[:,None] * gamma[None,:] + beta[None,:]
    tl.store(out_ptr + offs_p[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask)
```

Final kernel code:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(core_ptr, og_ptr, w_ptr, gw_ptr, gb_ptr, out_ptr,
                  B, N, H, D, n_n, inv_H, eps,
                  NUM_PB,
                  BLOCK_P: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
                  H_IS_FULL: tl.constexpr, P_EVEN: tl.constexpr, C_EVEN: tl.constexpr):
    pid=tl.program_id(0)
    pb=pid % NUM_PB
    b=pid // NUM_PB
    offs_p=pb*BLOCK_P+tl.arange(0,BLOCK_P)
    local_p = offs_p  # within b
    gp = b*n_n + offs_p
    if not P_EVEN: p_valid = offs_p < n_n
    if H_IS_FULL:
       off_h=tl.arange(0,BLOCK_H)
       # H == BLOCK_H
       if P_EVEN:
          c_tile=tl.load(core_ptr + b*H*n_n + off_h[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:])
       else: mask p_valid[None,:]
       cf=c_tile.to(tl.float32)
       sums=tl.sum(cf, axis=0); sumsq=tl.sum(cf*cf, axis=0)
       mean=sums*inv_H; var=sumsq*inv_H - mean*mean; var=tl.maximum(var,0); rstd=1/tl.sqrt(var+eps)
       gamma=tl.load(gw_ptr+off_h).to(tl.float32) # assume no mask; if H full but H maybe not power? H_IS_FULL and BLOCK_H power => H power of two? if H=64, yes.
       beta=...
       c_pt=tl.trans(cf)
       if P_EVEN: og=tl.load(og_ptr + gp[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:]).to(tl.float32)
       else: mask p_valid[:,None]
       sig=1/(1+tl.exp(-og))
       norm=(c_pt - mean[:,None])*rstd[:,None]*gamma[None,:]+beta[None,:]
       gated=(norm*sig).to(tl.bfloat16)
       for c0 in range(0,D,BLOCK_C):
          oc = c0+tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
          if C_EVEN:
             w_ct=tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:]) # [BC,H]
             w=tl.trans(w_ct)
             acc=tl.dot(gated, w)
             if P_EVEN: tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:,None]*D + oc[None,:], acc)
             else: mask p_valid[:,None]
          else:
             c_valid=oc<D
             w_ct=tl.load(..., mask=c_valid[:,None], other=0.0)
             acc=dot
             store mask p_valid & c_valid
    else:
       # generic two-pass
       sums=zeros; sumsq=zeros
       off_h_base=tl.arange(0,BLOCK_H)
       for h0 in range(0,H,BLOCK_H):
          oh=h0+off_h_base
          h_valid=oh<H
          if P_EVEN: mask=h_valid[:,None]
          else: mask=h_valid[:,None] & p_valid[None,:]
          c_tile=tl.load(core_ptr + b*H*n_n + oh[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
          sums += tl.sum(c_tile, axis=0)
          sumsq += tl.sum(c_tile*c_tile, axis=0)
       mean...
       for c0 in range(0,D,BLOCK_C):
          oc=...
          c_valid = oc<D if not C_EVEN else None
          acc=zeros
          for h0 in range(0,H,BLOCK_H):
             oh...
             h_valid...
             c_tile load...
             c_pt=tl.trans(c_tile)
             gamma=tl.load(gw_ptr+oh, mask=h_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
             beta=...
             og_mask = h_valid[None,:] & (p_valid if not P_EVEN else tl.full?) cannot easily. We can branch P_EVEN.
             if P_EVEN: og=load(..., mask=h_valid[None,:], other=0) else mask p_valid & h_valid
             sig...
             norm...
             gated...
             if C_EVEN: w_ct=tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + oh[None,:], mask=h_valid[None,:], other=0) else mask h_valid[None,:] & c_valid[:,None]
             w=trans
             acc += dot
          store...
```

Final kernel code skeleton:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(...):
    pid=tl.program_id(0)
    pb=pid % NUM_PB
    b=pid // NUM_PB
    offs_p = pb*BLOCK_P + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_P)
    gp = b * n_n + offs_p
    if not P_EVEN:
        p_valid = offs_p < n_n
    if H_IS_FULL:
        off_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
        if P_EVEN:
            c_tile = tl.load(core_ptr + b*H*n_n + off_h[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:])
        else:
            c_tile = tl.load(..., mask=p_valid[None,:], other=0.0)
        cf = c_tile.to(tl.float32)
        sums = tl.sum(cf, axis=0)
        sumsq = tl.sum(cf*cf, axis=0)
        mean = sums * inv_H
        var = sumsq * inv_H - mean * mean
        var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)
        gamma = tl.load(gamma_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)
        beta = tl.load(beta_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)
        c_pt = tl.trans(cf)
        if P_EVEN:
            og = tl.load(og_ptr + gp[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:]).to(tl.float32)
        else:
            og = tl.load(..., mask=p_valid[:,None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-og))
        gated = (c_pt - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None] * gamma[None,:] + beta[None,:]
        gated = (gated * sig).to(tl.bfloat16)
        for c0 in range(0, D, BLOCK_C):
            oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
            if C_EVEN:
                w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:])
                w = tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc = tl.dot(gated, w)
                if P_EVEN:
                    tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:,None]*D + oc[None,:], acc)
                else:
                    tl.store(..., acc, mask=p_valid[:,None])
            else:
                c_valid = oc < D
                w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + off_h[None,:], mask=c_valid[:,None], other=0.0)
                w = tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc = tl.dot(gated, w)
                if P_EVEN:
                    tl.store(..., acc, mask=c_valid[None,:])
                else:
                    tl.store(..., acc, mask=p_valid[:,None] & c_valid[None,:])
    else:
        sums=tl.zeros(...)
        sumsq=...
        off_h_b = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_H)
        for h0 in range(0,H,BLOCK_H):
            oh = h0 + off_h_b
            h_valid = oh < H
            if P_EVEN:
                mask = h_valid[:,None]
            else:
                mask = h_valid[:,None] & p_valid[None,:]
            c_tile = tl.load(core_ptr + b*H*n_n + oh[:,None]*n_n + offs_p[None,:], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
            sums += tl.sum(c_tile, axis=0)
            sumsq += tl.sum(c_tile*c_tile, axis=0)
        mean = sums*inv_H; var...
        for c0 in range(0,D,BLOCK_C):
            oc=...
            if C_EVEN:
                c_valid = None
            else:
                c_valid = oc < D
            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_P, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
            for h0 in range(0,H,BLOCK_H):
                oh...
                h_valid...
                if P_EVEN:
                   mask = h_valid[:,None]
                else: mask = h_valid[:,None] & p_valid[None,:]
                c_tile=load...
                c_pt=tl.trans(c_tile)
                gamma=tl.load(gamma_ptr+oh, mask=h_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                beta=...
                if P_EVEN:
                   og = tl.load(og_ptr + gp[:,None]*H + oh[None,:], mask=h_valid[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                else:
                   og = tl.load(..., mask=h_valid[None,:] & p_valid[:,None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                sig=...
                norm=...
                gated=...
                if C_EVEN:
                   w_mask = h_valid[None,:]
                else:
                   w_mask = h_valid[None,:] & c_valid[:,None]
                w_ct=tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:,None]*H + oh[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
                w=tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc += tl.dot(gated, w)
            # store
            if P_EVEN and C_EVEN:
                tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:,None]*D + oc[None,:], acc)
            elif P_EVEN:
                tl.store(..., acc, mask=c_valid[None,:])
            elif C_EVEN:
                tl.store(..., acc, mask=p_valid[:,None])
            else:
                tl.store(..., mask=p_valid[:,None] & c_valid[None,:])
```
In generic, `c_valid` might be undefined if C_EVEN true and store uses `elif P_EVEN` with c_valid? The `elif P_EVEN` only when C_EVEN false because if C_EVEN true first store branch? Conditions: if P_EVEN and C_EVEN -> no mask; elif P_EVEN -> means P_EVEN true, C_EVEN false, so c_valid defined. elif C_EVEN -> P_EVEN false, C_EVEN true, no c_valid needed. else -> both false. Good.
In W load, if C_EVEN false `c_valid` defined in outer c0. Good.

Potential issue: In proj, L/R h-major store uses `b_start*H*n_n + h0*n_n + offs_h[:,None]*n_n + local_offs[None,:]`. For b_start maybe int, H*n_n maybe int. In Triton scalar multiplication, if values exceed int32? <2^31. Good.

```python
"""
Fused Triton/PyTorch implementation of the AlphaFold3 TriMul forward pass.

Pipeline:
  1. Triton LayerNorm over the last dimension of the input.
  2. Fused Triton kernel for the five linear projections, sigmoid gating,
     mask multiplication, and direct store of left/right in h-major layout
     and out-gate in row-major layout.
  3. cuBLAS batched GEMM for the core TriMul contraction:
         C[b,h,i,j] = sum_k L[b,h,i,k] * R[b,h,j,k]
     This is implemented by viewing L/R as [B*H, N, N].
  4. Fused Triton kernel for final LayerNorm over H, out-gate multiplication,
     and projection back to the output dimension.
"""

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl


def _next_power_of_two(n: int) -> int:
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p *= 2
    return p


@triton.jit
def _ln_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    b_ptr,
    out_ptr,
    M,
    C,
    inv_C,
    eps,
    BLOCK_P: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_p = pid * BLOCK_P + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_P)
    p_valid = offs_p < M

    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    c_valid = offs_c < C

    mask = p_valid[:, None] & c_valid[None, :]
    x = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    x = tl.where(c_valid[None, :], x, 0.0)

    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_C
    xc = x - mean[:, None]
    xc = tl.where(c_valid[None, :], xc, 0.0)

    var = tl.sum(xc * xc, axis=1) * inv_C
    var = tl.where(var > 0.0, var, 0.0)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

    gamma = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    beta = tl.load(b_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    y = xc * rstd[:, None] * gamma[None, :] + beta[None, :]
    tl.store(out_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask)


@triton.jit
def _proj_fuse_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    mask_ptr,
    l_ptr,
    r_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    wl_ptr,
    wr_ptr,
    wlg_ptr,
    wrg_ptr,
    wog_ptr,
    M,
    H,
    D,
    n_n,
    n_hb,
    EVEN: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)

    pid_h = pid % n_hb
    pid_t = pid // n_hb

    p_start = pid_t * BLOCK_M
    offs_m = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    offs_p = p_start + offs_m

    b_start = p_start // n_n
    local_p0 = p_start - b_start * n_n

    h0 = pid_h * BLOCK_H
    offs_h = h0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)

    if EVEN:
        if HAS_MASK:
            m = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_p).to(tl.float32)
        else:
            m = tl.full((BLOCK_M,), 1.0, dtype=tl.float32)

        acc_l = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_r = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_lg = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_rg = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_og = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)

        for c0 in range(0, D, BLOCK_K):
            oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)

            a = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * D + oc[None, :])

            w_off = offs_h[:, None] * D + oc[None, :]
            wl = tl.load(wl_ptr + w_off)
            wr = tl.load(wr_ptr + w_off)
            wlg = tl.load(wlg_ptr + w_off)
            wrg = tl.load(wrg_ptr + w_off)
            wog = tl.load(wog_ptr + w_off)

            acc_l = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wl), acc_l)
            acc_r = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wr), acc_r)
            acc_lg = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wlg), acc_lg)
            acc_rg = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wrg), acc_rg)
            acc_og = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wog), acc_og)

        sig_lg = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_lg))
        sig_rg = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_rg))
        sig_og = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_og))

        left = (acc_l * m[:, None] * sig_lg).to(tl.bfloat16)
        right = (acc_r * m[:, None] * sig_rg).to(tl.bfloat16)
        og = sig_og.to(tl.bfloat16)

        local_offs = local_p0 + offs_m

        l_ptrs = l_ptr + b_start * H * n_n + h0 * n_n + offs_h[:, None] * n_n + local_offs[None, :]
        r_ptrs = r_ptr + b_start * H * n_n + h0 * n_n + offs_h[:, None] * n_n + local_offs[None, :]
        og_ptrs = og_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :]

        tl.store(l_ptrs, tl.trans(left))
        tl.store(r_ptrs, tl.trans(right))
        tl.store(og_ptrs, og)
    else:
        p_valid = offs_p < M
        h_valid = offs_h < H

        if HAS_MASK:
            m = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_p, mask=p_valid, other=1.0).to(tl.float32)
        else:
            m = tl.full((BLOCK_M,), 1.0, dtype=tl.float32)

        acc_l = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_r = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_lg = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_rg = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_og = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H), dtype=tl.float32)

        for c0 in range(0, D, BLOCK_K):
            oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)
            c_valid = oc < D

            a_mask = p_valid[:, None] & c_valid[None, :]
            w_mask = h_valid[:, None] & c_valid[None, :]

            a = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], mask=a_mask, other=0.0)

            w_off = offs_h[:, None] * D + oc[None, :]
            wl = tl.load(wl_ptr + w_off, mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            wr = tl.load(wr_ptr + w_off, mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            wlg = tl.load(wlg_ptr + w_off, mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            wrg = tl.load(wrg_ptr + w_off, mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            wog = tl.load(wog_ptr + w_off, mask=w_mask, other=0.0)

            acc_l = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wl), acc_l)
            acc_r = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wr), acc_r)
            acc_lg = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wlg), acc_lg)
            acc_rg = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wrg), acc_rg)
            acc_og = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(wog), acc_og)

        sig_lg = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_lg))
        sig_rg = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_rg))
        sig_og = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-acc_og))

        left = (acc_l * m[:, None] * sig_lg).to(tl.bfloat16)
        right = (acc_r * m[:, None] * sig_rg).to(tl.bfloat16)
        og = sig_og.to(tl.bfloat16)

        local_offs = local_p0 + offs_m

        l_ptrs = l_ptr + b_start * H * n_n + h0 * n_n + offs_h[:, None] * n_n + local_offs[None, :]
        r_ptrs = r_ptr + b_start * H * n_n + h0 * n_n + offs_h[:, None] * n_n + local_offs[None, :]
        og_ptrs = og_ptr + offs_p[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :]

        store_mask = h_valid[:, None] & p_valid[None, :]
        og_mask = p_valid[:, None] & h_valid[None, :]

        tl.store(l_ptrs, tl.trans(left), mask=store_mask)
        tl.store(r_ptrs, tl.trans(right), mask=store_mask)
        tl.store(og_ptrs, og, mask=og_mask)


@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(
    core_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    gw_ptr,
    gb_ptr,
    out_ptr,
    N,
    H,
    D,
    n_n,
    inv_H,
    eps,
    NUM_PB,
    BLOCK_P: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    H_IS_FULL: tl.constexpr,
    P_EVEN: tl.constexpr,
    C_EVEN: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    pb = pid % NUM_PB
    b = pid // NUM_PB

    offs_p = pb * BLOCK_P + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_P)
    gp = b * n_n + offs_p

    if not P_EVEN:
        p_valid = offs_p < n_n

    if H_IS_FULL:
        off_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)

        if P_EVEN:
            c_tile = tl.load(core_ptr + b * H * n_n + off_h[:, None] * n_n + offs_p[None, :])
        else:
            c_tile = tl.load(
                core_ptr + b * H * n_n + off_h[:, None] * n_n + offs_p[None, :],
                mask=p_valid[None, :],
                other=0.0,
            )

        cf = c_tile.to(tl.float32)
        sums = tl.sum(cf, axis=0)
        sumsq = tl.sum(cf * cf, axis=0)

        mean = sums * inv_H
        var = sumsq * inv_H - mean * mean
        var = tl.where(var > 0.0, var, 0.0)
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

        gamma = tl.load(gw_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)
        beta = tl.load(gb_ptr + off_h).to(tl.float32)

        c_pt = tl.trans(cf)

        if P_EVEN:
            og = tl.load(og_ptr + gp[:, None] * H + off_h[None, :]).to(tl.float32)
        else:
            og = tl.load(
                og_ptr + gp[:, None] * H + off_h[None, :],
                mask=p_valid[:, None],
                other=0.0,
            ).to(tl.float32)

        sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-og))
        gated = (c_pt - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * gamma[None, :] + beta[None, :]
        gated = (gated * sig).to(tl.bfloat16)

        for c0 in range(0, D, BLOCK_C):
            oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)

            if C_EVEN:
                w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:, None] * H + off_h[None, :])
                w = tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc = tl.dot(gated, w)

                if P_EVEN:
                    tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc)
                else:
                    tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc, mask=p_valid[:, None])
            else:
                c_valid = oc < D
                w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:, None] * H + off_h[None, :], mask=c_valid[:, None], other=0.0)
                w = tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc = tl.dot(gated, w)

                if P_EVEN:
                    tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc, mask=c_valid[None, :])
                else:
                    tl.store(
                        out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :],
                        acc,
                        mask=p_valid[:, None] & c_valid[None, :],
                    )
    else:
        sums = tl.zeros((BLOCK_P,), dtype=tl.float32)
        sumsq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_P,), dtype=tl.float32)
        off_h_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)

        for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
            oh = h0 + off_h_base
            h_valid = oh < H

            if P_EVEN:
                mask = h_valid[:, None]
            else:
                mask = h_valid[:, None] & p_valid[None, :]

            c_tile = tl.load(
                core_ptr + b * H * n_n + oh[:, None] * n_n + offs_p[None, :],
                mask=mask,
                other=0.0,
            ).to(tl.float32)

            sums += tl.sum(c_tile, axis=0)
            sumsq += tl.sum(c_tile * c_tile, axis=0)

        mean = sums * inv_H
        var = sumsq * inv_H - mean * mean
        var = tl.where(var > 0.0, var, 0.0)
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

        for c0 in range(0, D, BLOCK_C):
            oc = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
            if C_EVEN:
                c_valid = None
            else:
                c_valid = oc < D

            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_P, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)

            for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
                oh = h0 + off_h_base
                h_valid = oh < H

                if P_EVEN:
                    c_mask = h_valid[:, None]
                else:
                    c_mask = h_valid[:, None] & p_valid[None, :]

                c_tile = tl.load(
                    core_ptr + b * H * n_n + oh[:, None] * n_n + offs_p[None, :],
                    mask=c_mask,
                    other=0.0,
                ).to(tl.float32)

                c_pt = tl.trans(c_tile)

                gamma = tl.load(gw_ptr + oh, mask=h_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                beta = tl.load(gb_ptr + oh, mask=h_valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

                if P_EVEN:
                    og = tl.load(
                        og_ptr + gp[:, None] * H + oh[None, :],
                        mask=h_valid[None, :],
                        other=0.0,
                    ).to(tl.float32)
                else:
                    og = tl.load(
                        og_ptr + gp[:, None] * H + oh[None, :],
                        mask=h_valid[None, :] & p_valid[:, None],
                        other=0.0,
                    ).to(tl.float32)

                sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-og))
                normed = (c_pt - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * gamma[None, :] + beta[None, :]
                gated = (normed * sig).to(tl.bfloat16)

                if C_EVEN:
                    w_mask = h_valid[None, :]
                else:
                    w_mask = h_valid[None, :] & c_valid[:, None]

                w_ct = tl.load(w_ptr + oc[:, None] * H + oh[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
                w = tl.trans(w_ct)
                acc += tl.dot(gated, w)

            if P_EVEN and C_EVEN:
                tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc)
            elif P_EVEN:
                tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc, mask=c_valid[None, :])
            elif C_EVEN:
                tl.store(out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :], acc, mask=p_valid[:, None])
            else:
                tl.store(
                    out_ptr + gp[:, None] * D + oc[None, :],
                    acc,
                    mask=p_valid[:, None] & c_valid[None, :],
                )


def custom_kernel(data):
    # Unpack. The problem statement uses (x, mask, weights, config), but also
    # mentions the possibility of no mask.
    if len(data) == 3:
        x, weights, config = data
        mask = None
    else:
        x, mask, weights, config = data

    if not x.is_contiguous():
        x = x.contiguous()

    B, N, _, D_in = x.shape
    D = int(config.get("dim", D_in))
    H = int(config.get("hidden_dim", weights["left_proj.weight"].shape[0]))

    device = x.device
    n_n = N * N
    M = B * n_n
    eps = 1e-5

    # If a mask is supplied, materialize it as contiguous float32.
    has_mask = mask is not None
    if has_mask:
        mask = mask.to(torch.float32).contiguous()
        mask_ptr_arg = mask
    else:
        # Dummy pointer, not used when HAS_MASK is False.
        mask_ptr_arg = x

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1) LayerNorm over the input feature dimension D.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    norm_w = (weights.get("norm.weight", torch.ones(D, device=device))).to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    norm_b = (weights.get("norm.bias", torch.zeros(D, device=device))).to(torch.float32).contiguous()

    x_norm = torch.empty(x.shape, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)

    ln_block_c = _next_power_of_two(D)
    ln_block_p = max(16, 16384 // ln_block_c)
    ln_grid = (triton.cdiv(M, ln_block_p),)

    _ln_kernel[ln_grid](
        x,
        norm_w,
        norm_b,
        x_norm,
        M,
        D,
        1.0 / float(D),
        eps,
        BLOCK_P=ln_block_p,
        BLOCK_C=ln_block_c,
        num_warps=4,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2) Fused projections + gates + mask.
    #    Left/right are stored directly as [B, H, N, N] for cuBLAS bmm.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _bf16_weight(key: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        return weights[key].to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()

    w_l = _bf16_weight("left_proj.weight")
    w_r = _bf16_weight("right_proj.weight")
    w_lg = _bf16_weight("left_gate.weight")
    w_rg = _bf16_weight("right_gate.weight")
    w_og = _bf16_weight("out_gate.weight")

    L = torch.empty(B, H, N, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)
    R = torch.empty(B, H, N, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)
    OG = torch.empty(B, N, N, H, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)

    proj_block_m = 64 if (M % 64 == 0 and n_n % 64 == 0) else 16

    if H % 64 == 0:
        proj_block_h = 64
    else:
        proj_block_h = _next_power_of_two(H)
        if proj_block_h < 16:
            proj_block_h = 16

    if D >= 128 and D % 128 == 0:
        proj_block_k = 128
    elif D % 64 == 0:
        proj_block_k = 64
    else:
        proj_block_k = _next_power_of_two(D)
        if proj_block_k < 16:
            proj_block_k = 16

    proj_even = (M % proj_block_m == 0) and (n_n % proj_block_m == 0) and (D % proj_block_k == 0) and (H % proj_block_h == 0)
    n_hb = triton.cdiv(H, proj_block_h)
    n_tb = triton.cdiv(M, proj_block_m)

    _proj_fuse_kernel[(n_tb * n_hb,)](
        x_norm,
        mask_ptr_arg,
        L,
        R,
        OG,
        w_l,
        w_r,
        w_lg,
        w_rg,
        w_og,
        M,
        H,
        D,
        n_n,
        n_hb,
        EVEN=proj_even,
        HAS_MASK=has_mask,
        BLOCK_M=proj_block_m,
        BLOCK_H=proj_block_h,
        BLOCK_K=proj_block_k,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=3,
    )

    # Allow the input-normalized tensor to be freed earlier.
    del x_norm

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3) Core TriMul contraction with cuBLAS batched GEMM.
    #    For each h, C_h = L_h @ R_h^T.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    L3 = L.view(B * H, N, N)
    R3 = R.view(B * H, N, N)
    core_flat = torch.empty(B * H, N, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)

    torch.bmm(L3, R3.transpose(1, 2), out=core_flat)

    del L, R, L3, R3

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4) Fused final LayerNorm + out gate + linear projection.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    w_out = _bf16_weight("to_out.weight")  # [D, H]
    norm2_w = (weights.get("to_out_norm.weight", torch.ones(H, device=device))).to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    norm2_b = (weights.get("to_out_norm.bias", torch.zeros(H, device=device))).to(torch.float32).contiguous()

    out = torch.empty(B, N, N, D, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)

    final_block_p = 64 if n_n % 64 == 0 else 16
    final_block_h = 128 if H % 128 == 0 and H >= 128 else _next_power_of_two(min(H, 128))
    if final_block_h < 16:
        final_block_h = 16

    if D >= 128 and D % 128 == 0:
        final_block_c = 128
    elif D % 64 == 0:
        final_block_c = 64
    else:
        final_block_c = _next_power_of_two(D)
        if final_block_c < 16:
            final_block_c = 16

    num_pb = triton.cdiv(n_n, final_block_p)

    _final_kernel[(B * num_pb,)](
        core_flat,
        OG,
        w_out,
        norm2_w,
        norm2_b,
        out,
        N,
        H,
        D,
        n_n,
        1.0 / float(H),
        eps,
        num_pb,
        BLOCK_P=final_block_p,
        BLOCK_H=final_block_h,
        BLOCK_C=final_block_c,
        H_IS_FULL=(H == final_block_h),
        P_EVEN=(n_n % final_block_p == 0),
        C_EVEN=(D % final_block_c == 0),
        num_warps=4,
        num_stages=2,
    )

    return out
```
